The AI usage atlas.

Exhaustive local AI usage analysis

209,611 records • 28,336,367,703 tokens • $8,529.66 API-equivalent estimate

  • Scope: local coding-agent records attributed to Jevelopers-neatHack-2026. 955 selected source files; 951 contain billable-token records. Other users, machines, remote services and runtime API calls without local agent records are not covered.
  • Window: 2026-10-10T06:09:05.045000-04:00 through 2026-10-12T17:35:05.058000-04:00. All derived calendar tables use America/New_York. This is a snapshot of active logs, not a completeness guarantee.
  • Amounts are standard API-equivalent estimates using the preserved October 11 pricing snapshot, not invoices or subscription charges. Fast/priority and regional premiums are excluded. Assumed and mapped model rates are disclosed separately; an exact model row does not independently verify its price.
  • Cache savings is an uncached-price counterfactual, not money actually saved on a subscription. Reasoning tokens are a subset of output and are not added again. Cache write TTL fields may be unavailable; zero in an optional TTL field means no recorded allocation.
  • Repository attribution uses organization Git remotes, current or previously verified worktrees, and recognizable repository paths. Historical mappings preserve attribution for removed worktrees. Shared organization work remains unallocated. Session selection also uses explicit project-task evidence and linked children; mixed-project sessions may require manual allocation.
  • Prompt capture, raw-log archiving, credential reading, other profiles/drives, remote hosts and scheduled refresh remain off. 13 ambiguous Claude sources were excluded. No prompt text is included in these exports.
  • Codex counters: 101/104 main and 536/537 child sessions agree within 2%. 4 exceptions are listed in the counter section; live counters, branch segments and truncation can differ. These are validation flags, not corrections to force agreement.
  • The project scanner corrects rapid child-call replay undercount while preserving copied-session replay detection. 8 zero-token synthetic Claude records are excluded. Claude stats-cache predates this project and cannot independently reconcile this window.
  • Session counts use tool + host + recorded session ID. Claude child logs can reuse a parent ID, so session totals are not the number of agents. The complete source-segment appendix distinguishes log files. Session spans describe first-to-last records, not labor or compute duration.
  • Account labels are local pseudonymous groupings; identity confidence is low. Recorded plan names do not establish payer, invoice amount or subscription allocation. Actual spend, ROI, productivity, provider-wide totals and task-content analysis cannot be inferred from these records.
  • All 34 partition tables reconcile exactly to total calls and tokens and within one cent to event-level estimated cost. Percentiles use nearest rank. Per-call CSV includes every usage record; the PDF prints the 30 highest estimated-cost records and all session and source-segment aggregates.
  • Organization repositories with no directly attributed records: entire-checkpoints, Jevelopers-GetTicketStub.com-Submission. Absence is not proof of zero usage.
